- 2026-10-24: Chris Olave vs Tetairoa McMillan — Shmuel256: Track record. Olave isnt that old either. Tet is worth around the 1.02, only
- 2026-10-24: Carnell Tate vs Brock Purdy — Shmuel256: Not close here. Tate is worth less than tet mcmillan, or at best around the same (good clearly but not yet elite). Purdy is a set and forget qb1 thats worth 2 good firsts at least
- 2026-10-26: DeVonta Smith vs Jaxson Dart — BROsenzweig: Both of these values are WAY too low; each should be at least twice as valuable
- 2026-10-26: TreVeyon Henderson vs Isaiah Likely — Oliverwkw: Likely is TE2 on the year and still 26. Plus he's a giant
- 2026-10-26: Kenyon Sadiq vs Jordan Love — Oliverwkw: Sadiq was 1.8 and he's still worth 1.8
- 2026-10-26: Travis Kelce vs Blake Corum — Oliverwkw: Kelce is on the verge of retirement he's not worth more than a 4th
- 2026-10-26: Marvin Harrison Jr. vs Brenton Strange — Oliverwkw: Neither of these guys is worth more than a high 3, although both have an argument for 2.08 or so
- 2026-10-26: Drake London vs Lamar Jackson — Shmuel256: Im biased here admittedly but jackson’s a top 2 qb when healthy. London isnt a top 5 wr
- 2026-10-26: TreVeyon Henderson vs Isaiah Likely — Shmuel256: This years production difference is really stark. Giving up on hendo personally

- 2026-10-26: Quinshon Judkins vs Garrett Wilson — Shmuel256: Juskins seems average. So nothing exciting. Wilson has top 3 wr upside if they ever get a qb

- 2026-10-27: D'Andre Swift vs Cam Ward — Shmuel256: Ward hasnt shown anything at all in a year and a half. Starting to think hes a bust
- 2026-10-27: James Cook III vs Tetairoa McMillan — Shmuel256: Cook isnt too far off, but hes absolutely elite, not just good
